- **ready**: Artifact-anchored readiness gate. Derives the workflow phase of a PRD/Epic from its artifacts and verifies the ARD/spec/design artifacts justify it and the next transition; returns SUPPORTED / PARTIAL / NOT-SUPPORTED with a coverage roll-up. Never sets a status anywhere; never stops on an unmerged artifact (a readiness finding capping the verdict at PARTIAL) or a missing one (recorded as a coverage gap) — neither is a run-stopping gate. Commits its `_readiness.md` deliverable only via consent, never automatically. Gates on the Opus readiness-reviewer. ([ihudak/ihudak-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ihudak/ihudak-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-workflows/commands/ready.md) · ★2)
